  How to use 1-min signals:
    1. Find today's weekday
    2. Look up your currency pair
    3. At signal time (candle time), open CALL or PUT
    4. Expiry = 1 minute(s)
    5. 100% win rate — all days closed same direction
  ────────────────────────────────────────────────
